# Test Cases — US020 Receipt Reconciliation (four-way) · v4 · EN

> **This is the ENGLISH VARIANT of `Test_Cases_US020_VI_v4.md`, not a new version.** Same `v4`, same
> ids, same priorities, same browsers, same execution statuses, same blockers — titles and prose are
> translated (`R-LANG`, `R-NAME`). The Vietnamese file remains the base artefact: a behaviour change
> is made there first and re-exported here, never the other way round.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Module | Settlement · Inventory |
| US ID | US020 |
| Function | Receipt reconciliation — four-way |
| Build (SRS version) | v3.10 |
| SRS source | `SRS_Receipt_Reconciliation_US020_v3.10.md` |
| Prototype source | `PROTOTYPE_US020_ReceiptReconciliation_v2.3.html` |
| Blueprint source | `BLUEPRINT_Berriz_Settlement_Inventory_v1.32.md` |
| Coverage Matrix | `output/coverage-matrix/US020/CM_US020_VI_v4.md` |
| UI Mapping | `output/US020/US020_UIMapping.md` — 233 rows, the dictionary every Korean string below is quoted from |

## ⚠ Risks carried by this suite

1. 🟡 **The SRS is still Draft** — `v3.10` (28/09, `main` `f98c6580`). Prototype `v2.3` does not draw banner `118`, the `ORIGINAL_IN_GROUP` sentence or the new `LED-1120` wording — ⏳ in `Remark`. **`v3.10` states the wrong figure for an unattached correction** (it says `BR-1.V7`) — cases follow BA-3's clarification (`Q-26`: `BR-1.V6`); BA fixes it in the next version.
2. 🔴 **Twenty-one `LED-11xx` codes carry `[đề xuất]`** — not necessarily in the source yet. Screen cases assert the **string**; `[API]` cases assert the **code**, and follow any rename by Dev.
3. 🟢 **Period-lock endpoint #16 is in contract `0.5.1` (`x-status: live`, dev/qa/local)** — 26 locked-period cases move to `Ready`. Each case locks its own past month because **there is no unlock**; #16 does not read the pass signal.
4. ⚪ **The baseline is SRS `v3.10` for now** (owner 29/09). Cases resting on an **open question** (unconfirmed requirement) are **removed**: 20 statement cases (`OQ-133`) — listed under *Changed in v4*. Cases of an **agreed but undeveloped** requirement stay `N/A`: 15 permission cases · 2 cases (`TC_US020_099`, `TC_US020_109`) per `Q-26`.
5. ⚪ **Permission cases are `N/A` — out of the current test scope** (owner decision AnhTTT2, 28/09): every role is the same on the system and permission code `INVENTORY_RECONCILIATION` is not issued (`REPLY_QA` §2.3). The 15 `[Permission]` cases keep their content and come back when the code exists.
6. 🟠 **Ready rests on the owner's confirmation (2026-09-26) that `US062` and `US068` are deployed** — not measured by QA on the build.
7. 🟢 **Cases depending on `US003` are RUNNABLE** — `US003` is in Sprint 3; they execute in the SECOND round (they need accepted `TAX_INVOICE` batches on dev, one of them holding a `수정 전자세금계산서`).
8. 🟠 **Not customer-approved** — KE questions open (`OQ-125` · `OQ-133` · `OQ-169` · `OQ-183` · `OQ-208` · `OQ-209`); tolerance and reason-code cases assert **relations**, not constants.
9. 🟡 **Two places where the SRS differs from Dev, cases follow the SRS:** a corrective invoice attaches **only** when its original joins a group — not on arrival, not on opening a screen, not at lock time (`REPLY_QA` §1 · §2.4 say otherwise) · `LED-1119` (the contract says `CMN-4000`). Questions to Dev are **deferred** until Dev sends the API.
10. 🟡 **The no-price receipt is seeded by SQL** (`REPLY_QA` §3) — a QA decision; it needs write access to the `settlement` schema or Dev to run it.

## Scope notes

**This suite covers:** role-based access · the layout and labels of three screens *(group list · group
detail · build group)* and two overlays · filtering and pagination · building a group · four-leg
judgement · the two-tier tolerance ladder · product linking · recording reasons · the group status
lifecycle · the signal sent to period close · responsive behaviour · and seven edge groups.

<!-- history -->

**Review round of 2026-09-22 — 12 cases added.** Re-reading `BR-1`…`BR-17`, `BR-UI-1`…`BR-UI-11`, the
60 components and the 9 error codes against SRS `v3.2` found **eleven coverage units recorded as
`Covered` whose assertion was in fact missing** — mostly because the Inventory counts **one table as
ONE coverage unit**, so a table carrying four separate rules reads as covered the moment one case
checks its column list. `TC_US020_250`–`_189` fill exactly those holes. In the same pass
`TC_US020_208` was corrected: it used to assert that the document-side total was the algebraic sum
**including the statement**, whereas component `81` states that the document side **sums tax invoices
only** — adding the statement would double the document side and make every group holding one out by
its own value.

<!-- /history -->

**This suite does NOT cover, with the reason for each:**

- **Building data on upstream screens.** Receipts, collected documents, threshold entries and reason
  codes are created through the screens of `US105`, `US003`, `US068` and `US062`. The recipe for each
  lives in `working/extracted-requirements/US020/UPSTREAM_INPUTS_US020.md`; the behaviour of those
  screens belongs to their owning US, not to this one.
- **A group spanning two periods** *(risk 3)* — no source describes the expected behaviour, so writing
  a case now would mean inventing an expectation.
- **Period close and ledger generation** — that belongs to `US019` and `US023`. This US only emits a
  signal, and two `[Signal]` cases check that the signal blocks nothing by itself.
- **The outbound direction** — one `[QA]` case asserts that this screen never touches outbound; the
  rest of outbound is not in scope here.
- **Splitting one difference into parts** *(`BR-17.V3` · `LED-1107` · string `UM-069`)* — **out of scope
  for this delivery, and that is a recorded decision, not an omission.** The two coverage units sit in
  the Coverage Matrix at `Skipped` *(`MX-US020-057` · `MX-US020-120`)*. The reason: the split form needs
  a reason code **per part**, which comes from the very `US062` master that `A-13` is waiting on. BA
  *(BA-1)* answered `Q-12` on 21/09. **To be raised again when `A-13` is implemented.**

**Case order** follows the category, in the same order as the Coverage Matrix Scenario List. The category list is the **CLOSED 12-label list** of `testcase-rules.md` §3 *(closed 2026-09-23)*: seven core labels plus five major-action ones. This suite uses **seven** — `[Permission]` · `[UI]` · `[List]` · `[API]` · `[Create]` · `[Detail]` · `[Responsive]`. ⚠️ **This was a full relabel:** the previous version carried 14 labels, **ten of them invented** *(`[Judgement]` alone held 55 cases)*. A category is named after the **SCREEN**, never after the kind of rule — the test is whether a tester reading it knows which screen to open. **No case was added or removed**; the order changed.

<!-- history -->

## Test data preparation

> **Why this block lives in the case file** *(the `US080` pattern)*: the preconditions of 282 cases reduce to about 35 reusable data states; writing how to build each one once here is cheaper than repeating it on every row, and keeps the *one US, two main artefacts* rule — there is no separate `TEST_DATA` file.
>
> 🔑 **Every precondition line ends with a `` `[X]` `` label** pointing at exactly one row below. `S…` codes are the **State Inventory** codes of `CM_US020_VI_v4.md`. A label names **what must exist before the run** — a *state*, not a step. The concrete values (receipt numbers, approval numbers, amounts, months) stay in each case.
>
> Sources: `input/API/US020/openapi_US020-v3.10 1.yaml` (`0.5.1-provisional`) · `REPLY_QA_US020_2026-09-27 (1).md` §1 · §3.

### Locked months — one purpose per month, **there is no unlock**

> A lock record is never deleted (`BR-11.V3`), so a month locked by mistake is lost for every other case. A shared month serves **read-only** cases only. A month marked *not locked* is one where the case expects the lock to be **refused**.

### Data-building calls

```bash
B=https://<dev-or-qa-host>/api/v1   # dev / qa environment — #16 is NOT on cbt / prod

# S32 / S10 — lock a past month (use exactly the month in the table above)
curl -X POST $B/test-support/reconciliation-periods/lock -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
     -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"period":"2025-01"}'
# → 204 · 409 LED-1102 (already locked) · 409 LED-1108 / LED-1110 (a group cannot be locked — nothing is written)

# attach / detach a document (when faster than the UI)
curl -X POST   $B/reconciliation-groups/$GID/documents -d '{"approvalNos":["20260905-AAA"]}'
curl -X DELETE $B/reconciliation-groups/$GID/documents/$DOC_ID   # DOC_ID = documents[].id of GET /reconciliation-groups/$GID
```

```sql
-- S25 — no-price receipt: run BEFORE the group is built; a receipt with damaged goods has TWO incomings — run for both
UPDATE incoming_item SET purchase_price = NULL WHERE incoming_id = :incomingId;
```

⚠️ Building the group **before** clearing the price changes nothing (the price is read at build) — disband and rebuild. This block holds no delete-data statement and none may be added: a finished group is **disbanded** (`DELETE /reconciliation-groups/{id}`), a locked month is **retired**, never rebuilt.

## Summary

**Total TCs:** 282

That is exactly what `BR-UI-12` exists to stop: *a filter the system cannot apply must not look as though it was applied.* | — |
